# Commentary v3 micro finding review

You are the fresh micro-scope decision agent for **29:66**. This is a
linguistic reading task, not a prose-length or schema-compliance exercise.
Inspect the complete supplied micro evidence independently. Do not read an
existing commentary, a previous decision ledger, another scope response, or
repository material outside this prompt.

Your question is:

**What must a final commentary carry so that the ayah's local linguistic work
does not disappear?**

## Discovery posture

Err on the side of revelation, not suppression. Surface every meaningful image,
physical scene, relation, tension, ambiguity, sound-pattern, and surprising
lexical pressure that the supplied language can genuinely open. A reading does
not need to be familiar, majority-supported, or present in a canonical
commentary. Novelty, strangeness, a sole attestation, conflict with another
eligible reading, or difficulty of exposition is never by itself a reason to
reject it.

Uncertainty changes the label and containment, not whether a real contact is
allowed into review. When a carrier, a distinctive semantic facet, and an
independent local trigger make a consequential contact, preserve it at least as
`exploratory`. Do not protect the future prose by thinning the finding set.

This expansive posture does not license free association. Mere branch
availability is not a finding. Name the exact surface carrier, the exact facet
being activated, the independent word, syntax, or image that activates it, and
the new reader-visible consequence. If one of those edges fails, identify that
specific failure.

## Micro responsibility

Begin from the complete Arabic surface. Account for every surface word and
every meaningful morpheme, including transparent items that need no independent
finding. Review local grammar, syntax, lexical precision, form, sound when
evidenced, recurrence, ambiguity, and grounded secondary lexical pressure.

Use `primary_floor` as the supplied plain propositional footing before adding
local pressures. It is not a verdict: preserve what it plainly says while
freely recasting it into natural Turkish.

`commentary_obligation: ledger_only` marks carrier or provenance material. It
must be reviewed but cannot independently create a finding; it becomes finding
evidence only when a real linguistic contact supplies the missing trigger and
reader payoff.

The supplied candidates are a review docket, not an accepted list and not a
quota. Review every candidate. You may recover a new finding when the supplied
evidence contains a materially distinct local mechanism or reader payoff that
the docket missed.

The packet's `hft_evidence.assigned_records` are focus-only HFT nominations,
and each is a mandatory-review candidate with no presumptive outcome. Its exact
raw payload and available anchor Arabic are supplied by its sole `support_id`.
Review every one. Source binding, packet scope, confidence, or a missing
independent branch-registry entry may limit certainty, but may never decide the
outcome. Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only; HFT-stated segmentation,
word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless
independently supplied in the packet. If you accept, narrow, refer, or represent
an HFT item as an exact duplicate, carry its support ID and preserve its source
containment, live alternatives, analogy limits, and any “not a lexical gloss”
boundary. A duplicate target must preserve the evidence union.

Conduct one linear screen of every supplied focus branch. This is not a request
to pair every branch with every other branch. For each branch, ask whether its
distinctive definition or facet meets both its actual focus-ayah carrier and an
independent local trigger. “Independent” means independent of the branch
definition, not necessarily a second surface token: morphology, valency,
syntax, discourse contrast, recurrence, or an evidenced sound pattern can
supply the trigger. Record `nominated`, `no_independent_trigger`, or
`scope_referral`. A gloss or branch ID without its distinctive semantic content
does not count as review or coverage.

A multi-facet branch is not reviewed by naming one representative facet.
`review_facets` is the packet's normalized authoritative facet list, including
stable IDs for older source images that lacked numbered concept-map facets.
Silently test every supplied facet against the local evidence, then record every
tested facet ID with only `contact`, `no_independent_trigger`, or
`scope_referral` in that branch's screen row. The statement stays in the
evidence packet and need not be copied into the response. Emit contact
opportunities only for complete plausible contacts; an unactivated facet may
remain in the terse tested ledger without becoming a finding. The supplied
`focus_root_occurrences` are same-root occurrences, not proof that a branch
sense is active.

For every nominated contact, make the image explicit enough that another agent
can recover it without reopening the dictionary. If a branch is cited by an
accepted finding, state what that branch uniquely contributes. A citation that
does not explain the facet is false coverage.

Accept or narrow a finding when it is locally grounded and changes what a reader
can understand beyond a competent translation. Prefer `narrow` plus an
`exploratory` label over rejection when the contact is real but its reach or
certainty is limited. Different, countervailing, or hard-to-explain readings
remain eligible. Canonical status, source count, confidence labels, vividness,
or anticipated prose length never decides.

Reject only when you can identify the failed edge: no actual surface carrier,
no claimed facet in the supplied branch, no independent trigger, no changed
reading or reader payoff, a genuine scope violation, or an exact semantic
duplicate of another accepted finding. For a duplicate, name the
accepted target, explicitly show that local anchor, mechanism, direction, and
reader payoff are all the same, and preserve the union of its evidence. Merely
saying that a finding is similar or already represented is insufficient.
`must_integrate`
material is never silently rejected; compatible items may share prose later.
Scope overreach justifies rejection only when no bounded micro core survives;
otherwise narrow the claim or issue a `scope_referral`.

Work in two internal phases. First answer the bold micro question and discover
the real carrier/facet/trigger/payoff contacts without drafting coverage rows.
Then serialize those completed judgments. Coverage rows verify a judgment; they
must not generate one. Before returning JSON, re-ask the bold question and add
any grounded local work the ledger-building pass nearly displaced.

Boundary example: valid coverage says that carrier `X`, facet `F002` (“a
boundary channels movement”), and the clause's explicit destination together
turn generic motion into constrained passage, with a stated reader payoff.
False coverage merely lists `root_x/B_y`, `F002`, or “boundary/path” without the
independent trigger and changed reading.

Do not write the commentary yet. Acceptance must be settled before prose
facility can influence it.

## Required response

Return one JSON object with:

All model-created stable refs in this response (`finding_ref`, `proposal_key`,
`contact_ref`, and `referral_ref`) must begin with `micro:` so their identities
remain unambiguous when the three independent ledgers are reconciled.

- `schema_version: "commentary-v3-scope-review-v2"`;
- `identity` containing `ayah_ref: "29:66"`, `lane: "micro"`,
  `lane_packet_sha256: "169bd7163149b52115b5b53850f8200fb27b0b5b44ae33c0c85315b82fd8c7df"`, and
  `authoring_request_sha256: "27bc1bcd01354cd77014fa71c0debd0d41f561b4d37f391740c59499933ee0c6"`;
- `ayah_ref: "29:66"` and `lane: "micro"`;
- `coverage_complete`, true only after every supplied candidate, every assigned
  HFT record, every surface word, and every supplied focus branch has been
  reviewed;
- `surface_coverage[]`, one row per supplied
  `focus_surface_evidence.word_rows` item, keyed by its exact
  `analysis_record_ref`, with `treatment` (`develop`, `integrate`, or
  `transparent`) and the accepted `finding_refs` that justify non-transparent
  treatment. Coordinates are internal and must not enter reader prose;
- `branch_screen[]`, one row per supplied focus branch, with `branch_ref`, its
  complete `tested_facets` as `{facet_id, result}` objects, where `result` is
  `contact`, `no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`, branch-level
  `result` (`nominated`, `no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`), any
  `contact_refs`, and a specific reason. Do not echo facet statements or full
  root-occurrence records already present in the packet. A `nominated` branch
  must land in an accepted finding or in a linked
  accepted/narrowed/represented/referred contact; it cannot be a positive
  orphan;
- `contact_opportunities[]`, one row per meaningful contact discovered, with
  stable `contact_ref`, `surface_carrier`, `distinctive_facet`,
  `independent_trigger`, `changed_reading`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`,
  `suggested_lane`, `disposition` (`accept`, `narrow`, `represented`, or
  `scope_referral`), the accepted `finding_refs` for an accepted, narrowed, or
  exactly represented contact, complete `support_ids`, and complete
  `branch_refs`. A failed attempted edge belongs in its candidate, branch, or
  facet ledger with the specific failure; it is not a contact and receives no
  `contact_ref`;
- `candidate_decisions[]`, one row per supplied candidate, with `candidate_id`,
  `decision` (`accept`, `narrow`, `reject`, or `scope_referral`), concise
  `reason`, `failed_edge` when rejected, related `accepted_finding_refs`, and
  nullable `duplicate_of`, complete `excluded_branch_refs`, and
  `branch_exclusion_reasons[]` (`branch_ref`, `reason`). Use empty arrays when no
  branch was dropped. Do not repeat the candidate's original evidence arrays;
  they remain available by `candidate_id` in the packet;
- `scope_referrals[]`, one row for every candidate decision, branch screen, or
  contact that uses `scope_referral`, with stable `referral_ref`, `origin_ref`,
  `referred_lane`, `focus_carrier`, `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`,
  complete `support_ids`, `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, and `contact_refs`,
  plus a specific `reason`. A referral is lossless work for another lane, not a
  rejection shorthand;
- `new_findings[]`, a compact map for uncandidate discoveries, each with a
  stable `proposal_key`, its `accepted_finding_ref`, and exact `contact_refs`
  naming its originating contacts when contact-derived. Use an empty list for a
  purely surface, grammar, syntax, form, or sound discovery that needs no
  branch contact; put the finding content and evidence only in
  `accepted_findings`;
- `accepted_findings[]`, one row for every accepted existing or new finding,
  with `finding_ref`, complete `candidate_ids`, complete `proposal_keys`,
  `title`, `claim`, `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete
  `support_ids`, `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, `contact_refs`, and
  `branch_contributions`, and `epistemic_status`. Supply one or more distinct
  contributions for every cited `branch_ref`. Give separate rows when one
  branch activates multiple distinctive facets or independently anchored
  contributions; never flatten them into one generic row. Each row names the
  exact single `facet_id` from that branch's `review_facets`, its
  `distinctive_facet`, `surface_carrier`, `independent_anchor`, actual
  `contribution`, and `boundary`. The same facet ID must have result `contact`
  in that branch's tested-facet ledger;
- `friction_notes[]` for real ambiguity, evidence gaps, scope referrals, or
  decisions a reconciler should inspect.

Every supplied candidate and branch must receive an explicit disposition.
Every assigned HFT record is already one supplied candidate and therefore must
receive exactly one candidate decision; qualified metadata cannot erase it.
Every accepted or narrowed candidate must appear in `accepted_findings`.
An accepted finding cites only the support and branches its final claim actually
uses. When narrowing drops an original branch, record it in the owning candidate
decision's `excluded_branch_refs` and `branch_exclusion_reasons`; do not preserve
discarded branches as false semantic coverage. Every `scope_referral` disposition
must have one stable payload in `scope_referrals`.
Referred and rejected material remains visible in the decision ledger. Do not
rank accepted findings. Semantic completeness matters more than cosmetic JSON
regularity; never omit a reading merely to simplify the response envelope.

After returning the decision ledger, remain in this same conversation. A later
follow-up will supply the reconciled, locked micro set for prose preparation.

I 3mp imperfect subjunctive under the opening lām, denying or being ungrateful for the following gift complement\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:يَكْفُرُوا۟}} ({{tr:yakfurū}}) is not a standalone indicative: its missing final nūn shows the opening lām has drawn it into a purpose-or-result frame. The hidden plural subject keeps the rescued group from 29:65 in view, so the same people who received deliverance now cover over what was given. The root image makes the act more than abstract disbelief: {{ar:ك ف ر}} ({{tr:k-f-r}}) lets the denial feel like burying a gift. Its closed k-to-fricative/r release gives that first lexical beat an audible covered-then-released texture. Form I keeps that culpability direct, while the local sequence pairs this covering with divine giving, self-enjoyment, and the future knowing that will answer it; the exact denial-of-gift formula also recurs in 16:55.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ك ف ر}} ({{tr:k-f-r}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"covering, concealing, denying, rejecting, and ingratitude; locally narrowed to active denial of what was divinely given\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:يَكْفُرُوا۟}} ({{tr:yakfurū}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:1:paired-lam-cadence","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_aec5c6d1cd869a1a13c3","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"first lām sets up the paired lām clauses\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader hears the first short particle as the opening beat of a matched denial-and-enjoyment pair.\",\"reason\":\"The first lām is fused to its verb and is answered by the second lām clause, so form and sound both support the paired structure.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"MT-36816412\",\"QP-0fac45c9\",\"QF-1527192b\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:2:root-pair-and-echoes","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_b5adb46b89f8f2e89531","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"denial is paired with giving, enjoyment, and coming knowledge\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader sees the local verb as part of a larger pattern: denial is aimed at giving, paired with enjoyment, and answered by future knowledge.\",\"reason\":\"The row family supplies local pairings and concrete echoes, including the exact formula in 16:55 and the same-root continuation in 29:67; these support relation and recurrence without changing the local parse.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QI-4202b620\",\"QI-5b720128\",\"QI-a3df9fc8\",\"QE-ed0e6e9f\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:2:same-group-collective-agency","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_b7a02a5ea7e77e6dcb5b","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"the same rescued group performs the denial\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader sees that the people rescued in 29:65 remain the plural actors of denial in 29:66.\",\"reason\":\"The 3mp subject is morphologically present and discourse-linked to the prior ship-passenger group, so no new actor is introduced.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QG-630ae0af\",\"QF-eca815cc\",\"QB-e15353b0\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:5","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_bac1a27df88084823893","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"Form IV perfect first-person plural bestowal with 3mp recipient suffix; the given thing is open through the relative phrase\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمْ}} ({{tr:ātaynāhum}}) completes the open {{ar:مَا}} ({{tr:mā}}) but still refuses to itemize the gift; its sharp hamza onset marks the arrival of the defining gift verb after that open relative. The verb names divine agency and the same rescued recipients in one form: {{ar:نَا}} ({{tr:nā}}) as giver and {{ar:هُمْ}} ({{tr:hum}}) as recipients. Its Form IV bestowal makes the root's coming-image directional, as though the benefit was made to arrive to them, while the local syntax keeps the selected sense as giving rather than bare arrival. That is why the preceding denial becomes ingratitude, and why the later self-enjoyment looks like consumption of what had first been received. The exact denial-of-gift formula is also attested in 16:55, and the same-surah giving field around 29:47 and 29:49 broadens rather than specifies the gift.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:أ ت ي}} ({{tr:ʾ-t-y}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"coming, bringing, reaching, and giving; locally selected as divine bestowal with a caused-arrival image\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمْ}} ({{tr:ātaynāhum}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:11","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_be9764071c950988c48e","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"Form I 3mp imperfect indicative under sawfa; future knowing with no explicit object\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:يَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:yaʿlamūna}}) keeps the same plural group in view, but changes their role: the deniers and enjoyers become the ones who will know. The verb has no explicit object, so the threat remains open rather than limited to a named fate. Its retained final nūn marks indicative certainty after the two lām-governed subjunctives, and {{ar:سَوْفَ}} ({{tr:sawfa}}) fixes the imperfect as future realization. The root {{ar:ع ل م}} ({{tr:ʿ-l-m}}) answers {{ar:ك ف ر}} ({{tr:k-f-r}}): what was covered over becomes known and marked. Form I keeps that endpoint as direct realization. The {{ar:تَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:taʿlamūna}}) variant can turn the threat from report about them into direct address to you, while the recurring threat formula, including 6:67, 26:49, 26:227, and 78:4-5, lets this final word close the story as verdict; same-surah echoes at 29:41 and 29:64 add a local knowledge refrain.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ع ل م}} ({{tr:ʿ-l-m}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"knowing, recognizing, marking, and sign-related awareness; locally selected as open-ended future realization\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:يَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:yaʿlamūna}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:11:form-i-not-teaching","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_bff88299b84675712def","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"Form I makes realization direct, not instruction\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that the endpoint is their coming to know, not a teaching act they control or receive as instruction.\",\"reason\":\"The root family includes teaching forms, but the local Form I verb selects direct knowing.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QF-c15542e2\",\"QF-d9237d94\"],\"status\":\"narrowed\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:10","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_c0a1dda0f7a540a83be0","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"future particle projecting the knowledge verb into deferred but certain realization\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:سَوْفَ}} ({{tr:sawfa}}) stretches the final threat forward: the knowledge is delayed, but the delay does not make it uncertain. Because it stands as its own word between {{ar:فَ}} ({{tr:fa}}) and {{ar:يَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:yaʿlamūna}}), the ayah gives deferral a visible beat before closure. The formula {{ar:سَوْفَ يَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:sawfa yaʿlamūna}}) thus lands as a threat after denial and temporary enjoyment; the {{ar:لَسَوْفَ}} ({{tr:la-sawfa}}) variant only intensifies that certainty.\",\"root_display\":\"no lexical root\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:سَوْفَ}} ({{tr:sawfa}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:11:knowing-reverses-covering","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_c995c83ba9d96ed9e29b","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"knowledge answers the covering root\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader sees the ayah's semantic reversal: what was covered in denial becomes known and exposed at the close.\",\"reason\":\"The local ending places {{ar:ع ل م}} ({{tr:ʿ-l-m}}) opposite the opening {{ar:ك ف ر}} ({{tr:k-f-r}}), with accepted root pressure for knowledge, signs, and marked recognition.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QS-043471c3\",\"QS-992badb6\",\"QI-fa76e45b\",\"QE-3aad845f\",\"ME-1472eb28\",\"QY-b36dfdd6\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:11:open-object-threat","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_cfb82d67f39d654b3849","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"object omission keeps the threat open\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader feels the warning remain broader than any supplied object of knowledge.\",\"reason\":\"The local verb has no explicit object, and the guardrail warns against adding a specific object by default.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QG-11b27403\",\"MG-4084ac49\",\"QT-b2486673\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:4","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_d26bc3a77a9cdec731d7","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"relative pronoun governed by bāʾ; standardly the open thing given, with maṣdariyya pressure toward the act of giving\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:مَا}} ({{tr:mā}}) is the open hinge inside {{ar:بِمَا}} ({{tr:bi-mā}}): it belongs to the denial relation but does not yet say what the denied thing is. The reader must wait for {{ar:ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمْ}} ({{tr:ātaynāhum}}) before the object is defined as what God gave them. Because the pronoun is nonspecific, and because a maṣdariyya reparse can point to the act of giving itself, the accusation remains broader than a list of named blessings.\",\"root_display\":\"no lexical root\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:مَا}} ({{tr:mā}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:7","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_d2b8c571ae87b220ef45","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"second lām governing the enjoyment clause; standard purpose/result force with canonical command-force variant pressure\",\"prose\":\"The second {{ar:لِ}} ({{tr:li}}) mirrors the opening particle and places {{ar:يَتَمَتَّعُوا۟}} ({{tr:yatamattaʿū}}) in the same dependent frame as denial. The canonical sukūn-lām variant can make the clause sound like a command, but as threat-shaped permission rather than harmless invitation. The visible {{ar:وَلِ}} ({{tr:wa-li}}) stack layers coordination and governance, and the repeated lām beat prepares the break when {{ar:فَسَوْفَ}} ({{tr:fa-sawfa}}) opens the final warning.\",\"root_display\":\"no lexical root\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:لِ}} ({{tr:li}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:8:same-subject-subjunctive-objectless","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_da520fddb05f4f74d090","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"objectless subjunctive enjoyment shares the plural subject\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that the same group now takes unspecified enjoyment inside the same dependent lām frame.\",\"reason\":\"The verb has 3mp agreement, lacks final nūn under lām governance, and has no explicit object in the local frame.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QG-466c909f\",\"QG-543e1671\",\"QG-c370d0c7\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:5:divine-agent-human-recipients","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_dcd6af64ccbbf774f90a","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"one word separates giver and recipients\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader sees divine agency and the same rescued recipients compressed into one verb form.\",\"reason\":\"The first-person plural subject and 3mp object suffix are both morphologically present, and the suffix chain keeps the rescued group continuous.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QG-2809e1cf\",\"QS-636372a4\",\"QF-c9be6e90\",\"QF-eb8e2765\",\"QB-db4e41eb\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:9","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_de3156ca3cbd168552b2","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"fāʾ introducing the threatened result clause; causal, explanatory, and resumptive pressure\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:فَ}} ({{tr:fa}}) is the hinge where the ayah stops adding coordinated actions and turns to verdict. It can sound causal, explanatory, or resumptive: denial and enjoyment lead to knowledge, and the warning is also announced as a fresh threatening conclusion. Fused into {{ar:فَسَوْفَ}} ({{tr:fa-sawfa}}), the single-letter pivot moves directly from two lām-governed subjunctives to future indicative certainty, so {{ar:يَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:yaʿlamūna}}) answers both prior actions.\",\"root_display\":\"no lexical root\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:فَ}} ({{tr:fa}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:3:denial-complement","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_e9bbace8e44e934ef123","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"bāʾ targets the denied gift\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that denial cannot close on itself; the preposition immediately opens it toward what was given.\",\"reason\":\"The preposition governs {{ar:مَا}} ({{tr:mā}}) and attaches the denial verb to its specific complement field.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QG-7edf6878\",\"QT-23829133\",\"QF-d018e841\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:2:governed-subjunctive-scope","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_f40265edc3213d20b1c7","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"subjunctive denial governs the gift complement\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that the denial is grammatically dependent and reaches through the following prepositional gift phrase.\",\"reason\":\"The verb lacks final nūn under the preceding lām and takes the following {{ar:بِمَا ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمْ}} ({{tr:bi-mā ātaynāhum}}) as its complement field.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QG-48d3d5ca\",\"QG-dfddd7f3\",\"MG-be3d3318\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:2:covering-gift-ingratitude","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_f76a00f2639753d5bc6b","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"covering root makes denial of gift into ingratitude\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that the denial is pictured as covering or burying what has been given, not merely holding a wrong belief.\",\"reason\":\"The local object is what was given, while the accepted root field supports covering, denial, and ingratitude; unrelated root branches are not activated locally.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QS-7b545f0b\",\"QS-cc298d05\",\"QS-dce952f0\",\"MS-dc7fc12d\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:66:8","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_fc179804d797fb33b9d6","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"Form V 3mp imperfect subjunctive under the second lām; self-directed, objectless enjoyment\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:يَتَمَتَّعُوا۟}} ({{tr:yatamattaʿū}}) shares the lām-governed subjunctive frame and the same hidden plural subject as {{ar:يَكْفُرُوا۟}} ({{tr:yakfurū}}). It has no explicit object, so the enjoyment is left self-contained and unspecified. Form V makes the action self-directed: after divine bestowal, they take benefit for themselves. The {{ar:م ت ع}} ({{tr:m-t-ʿ}}) field adds temporariness, like provision consumed on a journey, and the following {{ar:فَسَوْفَ يَعْلَمُونَ}} ({{tr:fa-sawfa yaʿlamūna}}) judges that pleasure by deferred knowledge. Variant command-shaped readings sharpen the dare-like threat: one makes enjoyment a direct consequence-command, and another mediates it through command speech, while the standard wording keeps lām governance. The echo with 29:64 and recurring deny-enjoy contexts keeps the word inside a temporary-world field, and the movement from rescue story to verdict makes post-rescue self-enjoyment part of the sentencing turn.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:م ت ع}} ({{tr:m-t-ʿ}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"benefit, enjoyment, usable goods, temporary provision, and continuance;
